building_impl_complex: [AXIOM] MainOperations.BUILDING_FACT(Building, NOT(AND(OR(Car,Truck),Machine)))
residential_no_chimney_pipe: [AXIOM] MainOperations.IMPLICATION(Residential, NOT(OR(Chimney,Pipe)))
countryhouse: [AXIOM] MainOperations.EQUIVALENCE(CountryHouse, AND(Car, TiledRoof))
